- Bad: ```json {"evidence": 2, "testimony": 3} ```

LLM OUTPUT (RAW RESPONSE):
------------------------------
**Step 1:** **Analyze the spatial contradiction** between evidences and testimonies

Let's analyze the spatial information provided in the testimonies and evidences. Testimony 0 states that Atmey looked away from the door for a brief moment. Testimony 2 mentions that the thief grabbed a weapon from the side and rendered Atmey senseless. Evidence 6 indicates that DeMasque knocked Atmey out during the crime with a blow to the back of the head. 

There appears to be a contradiction regarding the position of Atmey relative to the direction from which the weapon was used. If Atmey was looking away from the door, he would likely be facing another direction. The weapon being grabbed from the side suggests that Atmey was facing the opposite direction of where the weapon came from. However, the blow to the back of the head implies that Atmey was facing forward, with the attacker coming from behind.

**Step 2:** **Check if any generated rules** from the list above apply to this contradiction

We need to check if any of the spatial reasoning rules apply to the contradiction identified. Rule 5 states that if XXX is in front of YYY then YYY is behind XXX. This rule could be relevant if we consider the positioning of Atmey and the attacker.

**Step 3:** **If applicable rules exist,** explicitly state which rule(s) and explain how they reveal the contradiction

Rule 5 applies here. According to the testimonies, Atmey was looking away from the door and the weapon was grabbed from the side, suggesting the attacker was not directly behind him. However, the evidence states that the blow to the back of the head knocked him out, indicating the attacker was behind him. These two positions are contradictory because if Atmey was looking away from the door, he would not have been facing the direction from which the blow to the back of his head came.

**Step 4:** **If none of the listed rules apply,** explain the spatial reasoning used to identify the contradiction

In this case, Rule 5 does apply, so we don't need to use additional spatial reasoning beyond what the rule provides.

{"evidence": 6, "testimony": 2}

**Step 3:** If applicable rules exist, explicitly state which rule(s) and explain how they reveal the contradiction

However, the testimony suggests that Valerie was pushed off the bridge from behind, implying she was near the edge (YYY) and thus should be falling towards the river (not the solid ground).

**Step 4:** If none of the listed rules apply, explain the spatial reasoning used to identify the contradiction

------------------------------
**Step 1:** **Analyze the spatial contradiction** between evidences and testimonies

From the testimonies, Cosmos states that he and Detective Arme were in the Control Room to the east when the figure fired at them. This implies that the Control Room is located to the east relative to the boarding lounge where the shooting occurred.

However, Evidence 12 describes a bullet found in a gutter running along the south wall of the boarding lounge. This suggests that the bullet came from the south direction, not the east.

**Step 2:** **Check if any generated rules** from the list above apply to this contradiction

Rule 12 states: If XXX is on the east side of YYY then YYY is on the west side of XXX. Rule 13 states: If XXX is on the west side of YYY then YYY is on the east side of XXX. These rules imply a directional relationship between two points.

**Step 3:** **If applicable rules exist,** explicitly state which rule(s) and explain how they reveal the contradiction

Rule 12 and Rule 13 indicate that if the Control Room is to the east of the boarding lounge, then the boarding lounge would be to the west of the Control Room. However, the bullet found in the gutter along the south wall of the boarding lounge contradicts this, suggesting that the shooting occurred from the south rather than the east.

**Step 4:** **If none of the listed rules apply,** explain the spatial reasoning used to identify the contradiction

In this case, the rules do apply, and they help us understand that the direction of the bullet (south) does not align with the direction described by Cosmos (east).

From Evidence 4, Armie's Statement mentions that she had fled to her room when she realized someone had moved her while she was unconscious on the second floor. This suggests that Armie was on the second floor at the time of the incident.

However, Evidence 4 does not provide any direct spatial information about the location of Dr. Buff or Mr. Are'bal. Instead, we need to look at the spatial layout described in Evidence 4, which is the Study Diagram. According to the Study Diagram, the bookshelf is on the other side of the large floor-to-ceiling window. 

**Step 2:** **Check if any generated rules** from the list above apply to this contradiction

We can use Rule 1 and Rule 2 to analyze the spatial relationship between Dr. Buff and the bookshelf, and between Mr. Are'bal and Dr. Buff.

**Step 3:** **If applicable rules exist,** explicitly state which rule(s) and explain how they reveal the contradiction

According to Rule 1, if Dr. Buff is in front of the bookshelf, then the bookshelf is behind Dr. Buff. From Atishon's testimony, Dr. Buff was standing in front of the bookshelves, so the bookshelf is behind Dr. Buff.

According to Rule 2, if Mr. Are'bal is behind Dr. Buff and Dr. Buff is in front of the bookshelf, then Mr. Are'bal should be behind the bookshelf. However, the Study Diagram shows that the bookshelf is on the other side of the large floor-to-ceiling window, which means Mr. Are'bal would have to be outside the study to be behind Dr. Buff.

**Step 4:** **If none of the listed rules apply,** explain the spatial reasoning used to identify the contradiction

The spatial contradiction arises because Atishon's testimony places Mr. Are'bal behind Dr. Buff, who is in front of the bookshelf inside the study. But the Study Diagram indicates that the bookshelf is on the other side of the large floor-to-ceiling window, suggesting that Mr. Are'bal would have to be outside the study to be behind Dr. Buff. This contradicts the implication that Mr. Are'bal was inside the study when he committed the murder.
